<file path="app/(tabs)/focus.tsx">
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, Switch } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { 
  saveFocusSession, 
  getFocusStats, 
  getTodayFocusTime, 
  FocusStats,
  setFocusModeActive,
  getBlockingSettings,
  saveBlockingSettings,
  updateBlockedApp,
  BlockingSettings,
  getSampleApps,
} from "../../lib/focus";
import { nativeContentProtection } from "../../lib/nativeContentProtection";
import { getStreak } from "../../lib/streak";
import { isLocked, hasPIN, setPIN, verifyPIN } from "../../lib/lock";
import PINModal from "../../components/PINModal";
import ProtectionActivationModal from "../../components/ProtectionActivationModal";
import { useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";

export default function FocusScreen() {
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(false);
  const [timeRemaining, setTimeRemaining] = useState(25 * 60); // 25 minutes in seconds
  const [selectedDuration, setSelectedDuration] = useState(25);
  const [sessionStartTime, setSessionStartTime] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<FocusStats>({
    totalSessions: 0,
    totalMinutes: 0,
    completedSessions: 0,
    currentStreak: 0,
  });
  const [todayMinutes, setTodayMinutes] = useState(0);
  const [streakDays, setStreakDays] = useState(0);
  const [blockingSettings, setBlockingSettings] = useState<BlockingSettings>({
    enableAppBlocking: true,
    enableNSFWBlocking: true,
    blockedApps: [],
  });
  const [showAppSettings, setShowAppSettings] = useState(false);
  const [systemWideActive, setSystemWideActive] = useState(false);
  const [systemWideAvailable, setSystemWideAvailable] = useState(false);

  // Security & PIN modal states
  const [pinModalVisible, setPinModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [pinMode, setPinMode] = useState<'set' | 'verify'>('verify');
  const [pinAction, setPinAction] = useState<'toggle_swp' | 'set_before_swp' | null>(null);
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pinExists, setPinExists] = useState(false);
  const [activationModalVisible, setActivationModalVisible] = useState(false);

  const params = useLocalSearchParams();

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStats();
    loadBlockingSettings();
    checkSystemWideProtection();

    if (params.autoStart === 'true') {
      if (params.duration) {
        const mins = parseInt(params.duration as string);
        if (!isNaN(mins) && mins > 0) {
          selectDuration(mins);
        }
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        startFocus();
      }, 300);
    }
  }, [params.autoStart, params.duration]);

  const checkSystemWideProtection = async () => {
    const available = nativeContentProtection.isAvailable();
    setSystemWideAvailable(available);
    
    if (available) {
      const status = await nativeContentProtection.getStatus();
      setSystemWideActive(status.active);
    }
    const hasPin = await hasPIN();
    setPinExists(hasPin);
  };

  const loadStats = async () => {
    const loadedStats = await getFocusStats();
    const todayTime = await getTodayFocusTime();
    const streak = await getStreak();
    setStats(loadedStats);
    setTodayMinutes(todayTime);
    setStreakDays(streak.currentStreak);
  };

  const loadBlockingSettings = async () => {
    const settings = await getBlockingSettings();
    if (settings.blockedApps.length === 0) {
      settings.blockedApps = getSampleApps();
    }
    setBlockingSettings(settings);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let interval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

    if (isActive && timeRemaining > 0) {
      interval = setInterval(() => {
        setTimeRemaining((time) => time - 1);
      }, 1000);
    } else if (timeRemaining === 0 && isActive) {
      completeSession(true);
    }

    return () => {
      if (interval) clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [isActive, timeRemaining]);

  const completeSession = async (completed: boolean) => {
    if (sessionStartTime) {
      await saveFocusSession({
        id: Date.now().toString(),
        startTime: sessionStartTime,
        endTime: new Date(),
        duration: selectedDuration,
        completed,
      });
      
      await loadStats();
      setIsActive(false);
      await setFocusModeActive(false);
      setSessionStartTime(null);
    }
  };

  const durations = [
    { label: "15 min", value: 15 },
    { label: "25 min", value: 25 },
    { label: "45 min", value: 45 },
    { label: "1 hour", value: 60 },
  ];

  const formatTime = (seconds: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const secs = seconds % 60;
    return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
  };

  const startFocus = async () => {
    setIsActive(true);
    setSessionStartTime(new Date());
    await setFocusModeActive(true);
  };

  const pauseFocus = async () => {
    setIsActive(false);
    await setFocusModeActive(false);
    await completeSession(false);
  };

  const resetFocus = async () => {
    if (isActive && sessionStartTime) {
      await completeSession(false);
    }
    setIsActive(false);
    await setFocusModeActive(false);
    setTimeRemaining(selectedDuration * 60);
    setSessionStartTime(null);
  };

  const toggleAppEnabled = async (appId: string, enabled: boolean) => {
    const updatedApps = blockingSettings.blockedApps.map(app =>
      app.id === appId ? { ...app, enabled } : app
    );
    const updatedSettings = { ...blockingSettings, blockedApps: updatedApps };
    setBlockingSettings(updatedSettings);
    
    const app = updatedApps.find(a => a.id === appId);
    if (app) {
      await updateBlockedApp(app);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleSWP = async (enabled: boolean) => {
    if (!enabled) {
      const locked = await isLocked();
      if (locked || pinExists) {
        setPinAction('toggle_swp');
        setPinMode('verify');
        setPinError(null);
        setPinModalVisible(true);
        return;
      }
      const stopped = await nativeContentProtection.stop();
      if (stopped) {
        setSystemWideActive(false);
      }
    } else {
      const exists = await hasPIN();
      setPinExists(exists);
      setActivationModalVisible(true);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmActivation = async () => {
    setActivationModalVisible(false);
    const started = await nativeContentProtection.start();
    setSystemWideActive(started);
  };

  const handleRequirePin = () => {
    setPinAction('set_before_swp');
    setPinMode('set');
    setPinError(null);
    setPinModalVisible(true);
  };

  const handlePINSubmit = async (pin: string) => {
    if (pinAction === 'set_before_swp' && pinMode === 'set') {
      const success = await setPIN(pin);
      if (success) {
        const { setLockEnabled } = require('../../lib/lock');
        await setLockEnabled(true);
        setPinExists(true);
        setPinModalVisible(false);
        const started = await nativeContentProtection.start();
        setSystemWideActive(started);
      } else {
        setPinError('Failed to set PIN. Try again.');
      }
    } else if (pinAction === 'toggle_swp') {
      const valid = await verifyPIN(pin);
      if (valid) {
        setPinModalVisible(false);
        const stopped = await nativeContentProtection.stop();
        if (stopped) {
          setSystemWideActive(false);
        }
      } else {
        setPinError('Wrong PIN. Try again.');
      }
    }
  };

  const selectDuration = (minutes: number) => {
    setSelectedDuration(minutes);
    setTimeRemaining(minutes * 60);
    setIsActive(false);
  };

  const progress = timeRemaining / (selectedDuration * 60);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={["top"]}>
      <ScrollView style={styles.scrollView} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {/* Header with Title and Streak Flame Counter */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <View style={styles.headerTextWrapper}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Content Calm</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
              Focus mode to minimize distractions
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.streakBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.streakFlame}>🔥</Text>
            <Text style={styles.streakText}>
              {streakDays} {streakDays === 1 ? 'Day' : 'Days'}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Stats Bar */}
        <View style={styles.statsBar}>
          <View style={styles.statItem}>
            <Text style={styles.statValue}>{todayMinutes}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>Today (min)</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statDivider} />
          <View style={styles.statItem}>
            <Text style={styles.statValue}>{stats.completedSessions}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>Completed</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statDivider} />
          <View style={styles.statItem}>
            <Text style={styles.statValue}>{stats.totalMinutes}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>Total (min)</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* ─── UNIONIZED FOCUS HERO CARD ─── */}
        <View style={styles.heroFocusCard}>
          {/* Circular Progress Timer Dial */}
          <View style={styles.timerCircle}>
            <View style={styles.progressRing} />
            {progress > 0 && (
              <View style={styles.progressContainer}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.progressLeft,
                    {
                      transform: [
                        { rotate: `${Math.min(progress * 2, 1) * 180}deg` }
                      ],
                    },
                  ]}
                />
                {progress > 0.5 && (
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.progressRight,
                      {
                        transform: [
                          { rotate: `${(progress - 0.5) * 2 * 180}deg` }
                        ],
                      },
                    ]}
                  />
                )}
              </View>
            )}
            
            <View style={styles.timerInner}>
              <Text style={styles.timerText}>{formatTime(timeRemaining)}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.timerLabel}>
                {isActive ? "Focus Active" : "Ready to Focus"}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Duration Selection Chips (seamlessly integrated inside hero) */}
          {!isActive && (
            <View style={styles.durationPillsRow}>
              {durations.map((duration) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={duration.value}
                  style={[
                    styles.durationPill,
                    selectedDuration === duration.value && styles.durationPillActive,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => selectDuration(duration.value)}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.durationPillText,
                      selectedDuration === duration.value && styles.durationPillTextActive,
                    ]}
                  >
                    {duration.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}

          {/* Unified Action Controls (Start / Pause / Reset) */}
          <View style={styles.heroControlsWrapper}>
            {!isActive ? (
              <TouchableOpacity 
                style={styles.heroStartButton} 
                onPress={startFocus} 
                activeOpacity={0.85}
              >
                <Feather name="play" size={18} color="#0f1718" style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                <Text style={styles.heroStartButtonText}>Start Focus</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.activeControls}>
                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.pauseButton} onPress={pauseFocus} activeOpacity={0.8}>
                  <Feather name="pause" size={16} color="#0f1718" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
                  <Text style={styles.pauseButtonText}>Pause</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.resetButton} onPress={resetFocus} activeOpacity={0.8}>
                  <Feather name="rotate-ccw" size={16} color="#e2a23b" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
                  <Text style={styles.resetButtonText}>Reset</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Content Protection Section */}
        <View style={styles.settingsSection}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Content Protection</Text>
          
          {/* Single Unified System-Wide Protection Toggle */}
          {systemWideAvailable && (
            <View style={styles.settingCard}>
              <View style={styles.settingIconContainer}>
                <Feather name="shield" size={20} color={systemWideActive ? "#10b981" : "#e2a23b"} />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.settingContent}>
                <Text style={styles.settingTitle}>System-Wide Protection</Text>
                <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
                  Filters explicit and harmful content across all browsers and apps on this device.
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.settingNote, systemWideActive && styles.settingNoteSuccess]}>
                  {systemWideActive 
                    ? 'Active — Device Protected' 
                    : 'Inactive — Tap toggle to enable'}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Switch
                value={systemWideActive}
                onValueChange={handleToggleSWP}
                trackColor={{ false: "#3e4a48", true: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4)" }}
                thumbColor={systemWideActive ? "#10b981" : "#94a4a2"}
              />
            </View>
          )}

          {/* Yellow Block Distracting Apps Button */}
          <TouchableOpacity 
            style={styles.yellowBlockAppsButton}
            onPress={() => setShowAppSettings(!showAppSettings)}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
          >
            <View style={styles.yellowButtonIconCircle}>
              <Feather name="slash" size={18} color="#0f1718" />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.yellowButtonTextWrapper}>
              <Text style={styles.yellowButtonTitle}>Block Distracting Apps</Text>
              <Text style={styles.yellowButtonSubtitle}>
                {blockingSettings.blockedApps.filter(a => a.enabled).length} apps selected for focus lockdown
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Feather 
              name={showAppSettings ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"} 
              size={20} 
              color="#0f1718" 
            />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {/* App Selection List (Expanded) */}
          {showAppSettings && (
            <View style={styles.appListContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.appListTitle}>Select apps to block during Focus Mode:</Text>
              {blockingSettings.blockedApps.map((app) => (
                <View key={app.id} style={styles.appItem}>
                  <Feather name="smartphone" size={16} color="#94a4a2" style={styles.appIcon} />
                  <Text style={styles.appName}>{app.name}</Text>
                  <Switch
                    value={app.enabled}
                    onValueChange={(enabled) => toggleAppEnabled(app.id, enabled)}
                    trackColor={{ false: "#3e4a48", true: "rgba(226, 162, 59, 0.5)" }}
                    thumbColor={app.enabled ? "#e2a23b" : "#94a4a2"}
                  />
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* Features Info */}
        <View style={styles.infoSection}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>During Focus Mode</Text>
          
          <View style={styles.infoCard}>
            <View style={styles.infoIcon}>
              <Feather name="bell-off" size={20} color="#e2a23b" />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoContent}>
              <Text style={styles.infoTitle}>Minimize Distractions</Text>
              <Text style={styles.infoDescription}>
                Stay mindful and present during prayer times and dhikr.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.infoCard}>
            <View style={styles.infoIcon}>
              <Feather name="clock" size={20} color="#e2a23b" />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoContent}>
              <Text style={styles.infoTitle}>Prayer Time Sync</Text>
              <Text style={styles.infoDescription}>
                Reminders notify you 7 minutes before each obligatory prayer.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={{ height: 40 }} />
      </ScrollView>

      {/* PIN Verification / Setup Modal */}
      <PINModal
        visible={pinModalVisible}
        onClose={() => {
          setPinModalVisible(false);
          setPinError(null);
        }}
        onSubmit={handlePINSubmit}
        mode={pinMode}
        error={pinError}
        title={
          pinAction === 'toggle_swp'
            ? 'PIN Required'
            : 'Set 4-Digit Security PIN'
        }
        subtitle={
          pinAction === 'toggle_swp'
            ? 'Enter PIN to disable System-Wide Protection'
            : 'You must set a 4-digit PIN before enabling protection'
        }
      />

      {/* Protection Activation Modal */}
      <ProtectionActivationModal
        visible={activationModalVisible}
        onClose={() => setActivationModalVisible(false)}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmActivation}
        hasPin={pinExists}
        onRequirePin={handleRequirePin}
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

<file path="app/(tabs)/index.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Switch,
  Animated,
  Dimensions,
  Alert,
  RefreshControl,
  Platform,
  Clipboard,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { getStreak, checkAndUpdateStreak, recordVPNDisabled } from '../../lib/streak';
import { isLocked, hasPIN, setPIN, verifyPIN } from '../../lib/lock';
import { nativeContentProtection } from '../../lib/nativeContentProtection';
import { getNextPrayer, formatTime, getPrayerTimes } from '../../lib/prayerTimes';
import { getCurrentLocation, getQuickLocation } from '../../lib/location';
import { requestNotificationPermissions, scheduleSalahNotifications } from '../../lib/salahNotifications';
import { loadSettings } from '../../lib/settings';
import { getFocusStats, FocusStats } from '../../lib/focus';
import PINModal from '../../components/PINModal';
import ProtectionActivationModal from '../../components/ProtectionActivationModal';

const { width: SCREEN_WIDTH } = Dimensions.get('window');

import {
  getDailyInspiration,
  rollNextInspiration,
  InspirationItem,
  AUTHENTIC_INSPIRATIONS,
} from '../../lib/dailyInspiration';

function calculateCountdown(prayerTimeStr?: string): string {
  if (!prayerTimeStr) return '';
  const [hStr, mStr] = prayerTimeStr.split(':');
  const h = parseInt(hStr, 10);
  const m = parseInt(mStr, 10);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return '';

  const now = new Date();
  const target = new Date();
  target.setHours(h, m, 0, 0);

  let diff = target.getTime() - now.getTime();
  if (diff < 0) {
    diff += 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  }

  const totalSecs = Math.floor(diff / 1000);
  const hours = Math.floor(totalSecs / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((totalSecs % 3600) / 60);
  const seconds = totalSecs % 60;

  const pad = (n: number) => n.toString().padStart(2, '0');
  if (hours > 0) {
    return `${hours}h ${pad(minutes)}m ${pad(seconds)}s`;
  }
  return `${minutes}m ${pad(seconds)}s`;
}

export default function DashboardScreen() {
  // Streak state
  const [streakDays, setStreakDays] = useState(0);
  const [streakStartDate, setStreakStartDate] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Protection state
  const [systemWideActive, setSystemWideActive] = useState(false);
  const [systemWideAvailable, setSystemWideAvailable] = useState(false);
  const [appLocked, setAppLocked] = useState(false);
  const [pinExists, setPinExists] = useState(false);
  const [activationModalVisible, setActivationModalVisible] = useState(false);

  // Focus state
  const [focusStats, setFocusStats] = useState<FocusStats>({
    totalSessions: 0,
    totalMinutes: 0,
    completedSessions: 0,
    currentStreak: 0,
  });

  // Prayer state
  const [nextPrayerInfo, setNextPrayerInfo] = useState<{ name: string; time: string } | null>(null);
  const [prayerCountdown, setPrayerCountdown] = useState<string>('');

  // Daily inspiration
  const [copiedInspiration, setCopiedInspiration] = useState(false);
  const [dailyInspiration, setDailyInspiration] = useState<InspirationItem>(AUTHENTIC_INSPIRATIONS[0]);

  useEffect(() => {
    getDailyInspiration().then(setDailyInspiration);
  }, []);

  const handleRollNextInspiration = async () => {
    const next = await rollNextInspiration();
    setDailyInspiration(next);
  };

  // PIN modal
  const [pinModalVisible, setPinModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [pinMode, setPinMode] = useState<'set' | 'verify'>('verify');
  const [pinAction, setPinAction] = useState<'toggle_swp' | 'unlock' | 'lock' | 'set_before_swp' | null>(null);
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Refresh
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);

  // Dev tap counter
  const devTapCount = React.useRef(0);
  const devTapTimer = React.useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  // Animations
  const streakScale = React.useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const cardFade = React.useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAllData();
    animateEntrance();
  }, []);

  // Live countdown timer ticker
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!nextPrayerInfo?.time) return;
    setPrayerCountdown(calculateCountdown(nextPrayerInfo.time));
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setPrayerCountdown(calculateCountdown(nextPrayerInfo?.time));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [nextPrayerInfo?.time]);

  const animateEntrance = () => {
    const useNative = Platform.OS !== 'web';
    Animated.parallel([
      Animated.spring(streakScale, {
        toValue: 1,
        useNativeDriver: useNative,
        tension: 50,
        friction: 7,
        delay: 150,
      }),
      Animated.timing(cardFade, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 500,
        useNativeDriver: useNative,
        delay: 300,
      }),
    ]).start();
  };

  const loadAllData = async () => {
    try {
      // Load streak
      await checkAndUpdateStreak();
      const streakData = await getStreak();
      setStreakDays(streakData.currentStreak);
      setStreakStartDate(streakData.startDate);

      // Load lock state
      const locked = await isLocked();
      const hasPin = await hasPIN();
      setAppLocked(locked);
      setPinExists(hasPin);

      // Load SWP status
      const available = nativeContentProtection.isAvailable();
      setSystemWideAvailable(available);
      if (available) {
        const status = await nativeContentProtection.getStatus();
        setSystemWideActive(status.active);
      }

      // Load focus stats
      const stats = await getFocusStats();
      setFocusStats(stats);

      // Load prayer times
      await loadNextPrayer();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error loading dashboard data:', err);
    }
  };

  const loadNextPrayer = async () => {
    try {
      const settings = await loadSettings();
      const location = await getQuickLocation();
      const lat = location?.latitude ?? 17.385044;
      const lng = location?.longitude ?? 78.486671;
      const { timings } = await getPrayerTimes(lat, lng, String(settings.calculationMethodValue));
      const next = getNextPrayer(timings);
      setNextPrayerInfo(next);

      // Schedule salah notifications
      const hasPermission = await requestNotificationPermissions();
      if (hasPermission) {
        await scheduleSalahNotifications(timings);
      }

      // Revalidate with fresh GPS in background if available
      getCurrentLocation().then(async (freshLoc) => {
        if (freshLoc && (Math.abs(freshLoc.latitude - lat) > 0.05 || Math.abs(freshLoc.longitude - lng) > 0.05)) {
          const freshData = await getPrayerTimes(freshLoc.latitude, freshLoc.longitude, String(settings.calculationMethodValue));
          const updatedNext = getNextPrayer(freshData.timings);
          setNextPrayerInfo(updatedNext);
          if (hasPermission) {
            await scheduleSalahNotifications(freshData.timings);
          }
        }
      }).catch(() => {});
    } catch {
      // Silently fail — prayer card will show fallback or hide
    }
  };

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await loadAllData();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, []);

  // --- PIN & Protection Flow ---
  const handleToggleSWP = async (enabled: boolean) => {
    if (!enabled) {
      const locked = await isLocked();
      if (locked || pinExists) {
        setPinAction('toggle_swp');
        setPinMode('verify');
        setPinError(null);
        setPinModalVisible(true);
        return;
      }
      const stopped = await nativeContentProtection.stop();
      if (stopped) {
        setSystemWideActive(false);
        await recordVPNDisabled();
        const streakData = await getStreak();
        setStreakDays(streakData.currentStreak);
      }
    } else {
      const exists = await hasPIN();
      setPinExists(exists);
      setActivationModalVisible(true);
    }
  };

  const handleStatusBadgePress = async () => {
    if (!systemWideActive) {
      const exists = await hasPIN();
      setPinExists(exists);
      setActivationModalVisible(true);
    } else {
      handleSetLock();
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmActivation = async () => {
    setActivationModalVisible(false);
    const started = await nativeContentProtection.start();
    setSystemWideActive(started);
  };

  const handleRequirePin = () => {
    setPinAction('set_before_swp');
    setPinMode('set');
    setPinError(null);
    setPinModalVisible(true);
  };

  const handleSetLock = () => {
    if (pinExists && appLocked) {
      setPinAction('unlock');
      setPinMode('verify');
      setPinError(null);
      setPinModalVisible(true);
    } else {
      setPinAction('lock');
      setPinMode('set');
      setPinError(null);
      setPinModalVisible(true);
    }
  };

  const handlePINSubmit = async (pin: string) => {
    if (pinAction === 'set_before_swp' && pinMode === 'set') {
      const success = await setPIN(pin);
      if (success) {
        const { setLockEnabled } = require('../../lib/lock');
        await setLockEnabled(true);
        setAppLocked(true);
        setPinExists(true);
        setPinModalVisible(false);
        // Start protection now that mandatory PIN is set!
        const started = await nativeContentProtection.start();
        setSystemWideActive(started);
      } else {
        setPinError('Failed to set PIN. Please try again.');
      }
    } else if (pinAction === 'lock' && pinMode === 'set') {
      const success = await setPIN(pin);
      if (success) {
        setPinModalVisible(false);
        setAppLocked(true);
        setPinExists(true);
        Alert.alert(
          'Protection Locked',
          'Your protection settings are now locked. Only someone with the PIN can disable them.',
          [{ text: 'Great' }]
        );
      } else {
        setPinError('Failed to set PIN. Please try again.');
      }
    } else if (pinAction === 'unlock' || pinAction === 'toggle_swp') {
      const valid = await verifyPIN(pin);
      if (valid) {
        setPinModalVisible(false);

        if (pinAction === 'toggle_swp') {
          const stopped = await nativeContentProtection.stop();
          if (stopped) {
            setSystemWideActive(false);
            await recordVPNDisabled();
            const streakData = await getStreak();
            setStreakDays(streakData.currentStreak);
          }
        } else if (pinAction === 'unlock') {
          const { setLockEnabled } = require('../../lib/lock');
          await setLockEnabled(false);
          setAppLocked(false);
          Alert.alert('Settings Unlocked', 'Protection settings are now unlocked.');
        }
      } else {
        setPinError('Wrong PIN. Try again.');
      }
    }
  };

  const handleCopyInspiration = () => {
    Clipboard.setString(`"${dailyInspiration.translation}" — ${dailyInspiration.source}`);
    setCopiedInspiration(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedInspiration(false), 2000);
  };

  const getStreakMessage = () => {
    if (streakDays === 0) return 'Start your pure journey today';
    if (streakDays < 3) return 'Every day counts, keep going!';
    if (streakDays < 7) return 'MashaAllah, building momentum!';
    if (streakDays < 14) return 'SubhanAllah, one week strong!';
    if (streakDays < 30) return 'AlhamduLillah, amazing discipline!';
    if (streakDays < 90) return 'You are a warrior of faith!';
    return 'Legendary. May Allah reward your steadfastness!';
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      <ScrollView
        style={styles.scrollView}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={onRefresh}
            tintColor="#e2a23b"
            colors={['#e2a23b']}
          />
        }
      >
        {/* ─── HEADER WITH SETTINGS & SHIELD PILL ─── */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
            <View style={styles.headerTitleRow}>
              <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Deen AI</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.statusBadge,
                  systemWideActive ? styles.statusBadgeActive : styles.statusBadgeInactive,
                ]}
                onPress={handleStatusBadgePress}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                accessibilityLabel={systemWideActive ? "Device Protected. Tap to manage PIN lock" : "Offline. Tap to enable protection"}
              >
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.statusDot,
                    systemWideActive ? styles.statusDotActive : styles.statusDotInactive,
                  ]}
                />
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.statusBadgeText,
                    systemWideActive ? styles.statusBadgeTextActive : styles.statusBadgeTextInactive,
                  ]}
                >
                  {systemWideActive ? 'PROTECTED' : 'OFFLINE'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>Your Islamic Digital Guardian</Text>
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.settingsIconButton}
            onPress={() => router.push('/(tabs)/settings')}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
            accessibilityLabel="Settings"
          >
            <Feather name="settings" size={20} color="#ffdda8" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* ─── STREAK HERO SECTION ─── */}
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            styles.streakHero,
            { transform: [{ scale: streakScale }] },
          ]}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.streakCircle}
            activeOpacity={0.9}
            onPress={() => {
              devTapCount.current += 1;
              if (devTapTimer.current) clearTimeout(devTapTimer.current);
              devTapTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
                devTapCount.current = 0;
              }, 1000);

              if (devTapCount.current >= 7) {
                devTapCount.current = 0;
                const handleUpdate = (val?: string) => {
                  const num = parseInt(val || '0');
                  if (!isNaN(num)) {
                    const { setStreakOverride } = require('../../lib/streak');
                    setStreakOverride(num).then(() => {
                      setStreakDays(num);
                    });
                  }
                };

                if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
                  const val = window.prompt('Developer Tools\nEnter new streak days manually:');
                  if (val !== null) handleUpdate(val);
                } else if (Platform.OS === 'ios') {
                  Alert.prompt('Developer Tools', 'Enter new streak days manually:', [
                    { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
                    { text: 'Update', onPress: handleUpdate },
                  ]);
                } else {
                  Alert.alert(
                    'Developer Tools',
                    'Dev override quick actions:',
                    [
                      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
                      { text: 'Reset to 0', onPress: () => handleUpdate('0') },
                      { text: 'Add 10 Days', onPress: () => handleUpdate(String(streakDays + 10)) },
                    ]
                  );
                }
              }
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="shield" size={24} color="#00ff66" style={{ marginBottom: 6 }} />
            <Text style={styles.streakNumber}>{streakDays}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.streakLabel}>
              {streakDays === 1 ? 'Day Clean' : 'Days Clean'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <Text style={styles.streakMessage}>{getStreakMessage()}</Text>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* ─── SYSTEM-WIDE PROTECTION CARD ─── */}
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            styles.swpCard,
            systemWideActive && styles.swpCardActive,
            { opacity: cardFade },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.swpHeader}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.swpIconContainer,
                systemWideActive && styles.swpIconContainerActive,
              ]}
            >
              <Feather
                name="shield"
                size={22}
                color={systemWideActive ? '#10b981' : '#e2a23b'}
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.swpInfo}>
              <Text style={styles.swpTitle}>System-Wide Protection</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.swpStatus, systemWideActive && styles.swpStatusActive]}>
                {systemWideActive ? 'Active — Device Protected' : 'Inactive — Device Exposed'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Switch
              value={systemWideActive}
              onValueChange={handleToggleSWP}
              trackColor={{ false: '#3e4a48', true: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4)' }}
              thumbColor={systemWideActive ? '#10b981' : '#94a4a2'}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Lock Button */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.lockButton, appLocked && styles.lockButtonLocked]}
            onPress={handleSetLock}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <Text style={styles.lockButtonText}>
              {appLocked ? 'PIN Locked - Tap to Unlock' : 'Tap to Set Uninstall PIN'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* ─── NEXT PRAYER & LIVE COUNTDOWN CARD ─── */}
        {nextPrayerInfo && (
          <Animated.View style={[styles.prayerCard, { opacity: cardFade }]}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.prayerCardInner}
              onPress={() => router.push('/(tabs)/prayer')}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <View style={styles.prayerTopRow}>
                <View style={styles.prayerBadge}>
                  <Feather name="clock" size={14} color="#e2a23b" />
                  <Text style={styles.prayerBadgeText}>NEXT AZAN</Text>
                </View>
                {prayerCountdown ? (
                  <View style={styles.countdownBadge}>
                    <Text style={styles.countdownBadgeText}>in {prayerCountdown}</Text>
                  </View>
                ) : null}
              </View>

              <View style={styles.prayerContentRow}>
                <View style={styles.prayerInfo}>
                  <Text style={styles.prayerName}>{nextPrayerInfo.name}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.prayerTime}>{formatTime(nextPrayerInfo.time)}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.prayerActionPill}>
                  <Text style={styles.prayerActionPillText}>Schedule</Text>
                  <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color="#e2a23b" />
                </View>
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </Animated.View>
        )}

        {/* ─── DAILY INSPIRATION (AYAH / HADITH) ─── */}
        <Animated.View style={[styles.inspirationCard, { opacity: cardFade }]}>
          <View style={styles.inspirationHeader}>
            <View style={styles.inspirationTypeRow}>
              <Feather name="book-open" size={15} color="#e2a23b" />
              <Text style={styles.inspirationTypeTitle}>
                Daily {dailyInspiration.type}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.copyButton, { marginRight: 8, paddingHorizontal: 8 }]}
                onPress={handleRollNextInspiration}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={13} color="#ffdda8" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.copyButton}
                onPress={handleCopyInspiration}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Feather
                  name={copiedInspiration ? 'check' : 'copy'}
                  size={14}
                  color={copiedInspiration ? '#10b981' : '#ffdda8'}
                />
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.copyButtonText,
                    copiedInspiration && { color: '#10b981' },
                  ]}
                >
                  {copiedInspiration ? 'Copied' : 'Share'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.inspirationArabic}>{dailyInspiration.arabic}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.inspirationTranslation}>
            "{dailyInspiration.translation}"
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.inspirationSource}>— {dailyInspiration.source}</Text>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* ─── QUICK ACTION TILES ─── */}
        <Animated.View style={[styles.quickActions, { opacity: cardFade }]}>
          {/* Focus Mode */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.actionCard}
            onPress={() => router.push('/(tabs)/focus')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={styles.actionIconContainer}>
              <Feather name="target" size={24} color="#e2a23b" />
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.actionTitle}>Focus Mode</Text>
            <Text style={styles.actionStat}>
              {focusStats.totalMinutes} min tracked
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {/* Masjid Finder */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.actionCard}
            onPress={() => router.push('/(tabs)/masjid')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={styles.actionIconContainer}>
              <Feather name="map-pin" size={24} color="#e2a23b" />
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.actionTitle}>Nearby Masjids</Text>
            <Text style={styles.actionStat}>1km radius locator</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* ─── QUICK IMAM AI PROMPT BANNER ─── */}
        <Animated.View style={[styles.imamPromptBanner, { opacity: cardFade }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.imamPromptInner}
            onPress={() => router.push('/(tabs)/chat')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={styles.imamIconCircle}>
              <Feather name="message-circle" size={22} color="#0f1718" />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.imamPromptTextWrapper}>
              <Text style={styles.imamPromptTitle}>Have a spiritual question?</Text>
              <Text style={styles.imamPromptSubtitle}>
                Ask Imam AI with Internet Archive verified citations
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Feather name="arrow-right" size={18} color="#e2a23b" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* Bottom spacer for comfortable scrolling above the tab bar */}
        <View style={{ height: 24 }} />
      </ScrollView>

      {/* PIN Modal */}
      <PINModal
        visible={pinModalVisible}
        onClose={() => {
          setPinModalVisible(false);
          setPinError(null);
        }}
        onSubmit={handlePINSubmit}
        mode={pinMode}
        error={pinError}
        title={
          pinAction === 'toggle_swp'
            ? 'PIN Required'
            : pinAction === 'unlock'
            ? 'Enter PIN to Unlock'
            : pinAction === 'set_before_swp'
            ? 'Set 4-Digit Security PIN'
            : 'Set Protection PIN'
        }
        subtitle={
          pinAction === 'toggle_swp'
            ? 'Enter PIN to disable System-Wide Protection'
            : pinAction === 'unlock'
            ? 'Enter your PIN to unlock settings'
            : pinAction === 'set_before_swp'
            ? 'You must set a 4-digit PIN before enabling protection'
            : 'Give this PIN to a trusted accountability partner'
        }
      />

      {/* Protection Activation Modal */}
      <ProtectionActivationModal
        visible={activationModalVisible}
        onClose={() => setActivationModalVisible(false)}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmActivation}
        hasPin={pinExists}
        onRequirePin={handleRequirePin}
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

    borderRadius: 8,
  },
  prayerActionPillText: {

  // ─── Daily Inspiration ───

    padding: 18,

    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  inspirationTypeRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 8,
  },
  inspirationTypeTitle: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: '#ffdda8',
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: 0.8,
  },
  copyButton: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 5,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 4,
    borderRadius: 6,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)',
  },
  copyButtonText: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: '600',
    color: '#ffdda8',
  },
  inspirationArabic: {
    fontSize: 20,
    lineHeight: 34,
    color: '#ffdda8',
    textAlign: 'right',
    marginBottom: 10,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
  inspirationTranslation: {
    fontSize: 13,
    lineHeight: 20,
    color: '#d6e2e0',
    fontStyle: 'italic',
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  inspirationSource: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: '#94a4a2',
    textAlign: 'right',
  },

  // ─── Quick Actions ───
  quickActions: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    gap: 12,
    marginTop: 14,
  },
  actionCard: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: '#0b2527',
    borderRadius: 16,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)',
    padding: 16,
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  actionIconContainer: {
    width: 44,
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 22,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(226, 162, 59, 0.12)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  actionTitle: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: '#f3f7f6',
    marginBottom: 2,
  },
  actionStat: {
    fontSize: 11,
    color: '#94a4a2',
  },

  // ─── Imam AI Banner ───
  imamPromptBanner: {
    marginHorizontal: 20,
    marginTop: 14,
  },
  imamPromptInner: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    backgroundColor: '#0c3033',
    borderRadius: 16,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: 'rgba(226, 162, 59, 0.2)',
    padding: 14,
  },
  imamIconCircle: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderRadius: 20,
    backgroundColor: '#e2a23b',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    marginRight: 12,
  },
  imamPromptTextWrapper: {
    flex: 1,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  imamPromptTitle: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: '#f3f7f6',
    marginBottom: 2,

  imamPromptSubtitle: {

    color: '#94a4a2',

<file path="app/(tabs)/masjid.tsx">
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, FlatList, ActivityIndicator, TouchableOpacity, RefreshControl, Linking, Platform, Alert, LayoutAnimation, UIManager, TextInput, Keyboard } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { getCurrentLocation, getQuickLocation, Coordinates } from "../../lib/location";
import { getNearbyMasjids, Masjid, formatDistance, resolveSearchCenterAndQuery, getCachedMasjidsSync, calculateDistance } from "../../lib/masjid";
import { subscribeToWarmup } from "../../lib/backgroundWarmup";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";

// Enable LayoutAnimation on Android
if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
  if (UIManager.setLayoutAnimationEnabledExperimental) {
    UIManager.setLayoutAnimationEnabledExperimental(true);
  }
}
// Configure your Google Form URL here (replace with your published Google Form link)
export const MASJID_UPDATE_FORM_URL: string = "";

export default function MasjidScreen() {
  const cached = getCachedMasjidsSync();
  const [masjids, setMasjids] = useState<Masjid[]>(cached || []);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!cached || cached.length === 0);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [currentLocation, setCurrentLocation] = useState<Coordinates | null>(null);
  const [selectedMasjidId, setSelectedMasjidId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");

  // Listen to background warm-up service
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToWarmup(() => {
      const fresh = getCachedMasjidsSync();
      if (fresh && fresh.length > 0) {
        setMasjids(fresh);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);

  const handleGlobalSearch = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    setLoading(true);
    await loadMasjids();
  };

  const getCleanedLocalQuery = () => {
    let query = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!query) return "";

    const prepositions = ["in", "near", "at", "around"];
    const neighborhoods = [
      "banjara hills", "jubilee hills", "mehdipatnam", "mallepally", "tolichowki",
      "charminar", "gachibowli", "secunderabad", "nampally", "amberpet",
      "himayatnagar", "begumpet", "kukatpally", "malakpet", "hitech city",
      "madhapur", "old city", "khairatabad", "somajiguda", "ameerpet",
      "yousufguda", "tarnaka"
    ];

    for (const nKey of neighborhoods) {
      if (query.includes(nKey)) {
        for (const prep of prepositions) {
          query = query.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${prep}\\s+${nKey}\\b`, 'g'), '');
        }
        query = query.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${nKey}\\b`, 'g'), '').trim();
      }
    }

    for (const prep of prepositions) {
      const match = query.match(new RegExp(`\\b${prep}\\s+([a-z0-9\\s]{3,20})$`));
      if (match && match[1]) {
        query = query.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${prep}\\s+${match[1]}\\b`, 'g'), '').trim();
      }
    }

    return query.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  };

  const filteredMasjids = useMemo(() => {
    const query = getCleanedLocalQuery();
    return masjids
      .filter(masjid => {
        if (!query) return true;
        return (
          masjid.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          masjid.address.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          (masjid.vicinity && masjid.vicinity.toLowerCase().includes(query)) ||
          (masjid.school && masjid.school.toLowerCase().includes(query))
        );
      })
      .map(masjid => {
        if (currentLocation) {
          return {
            ...masjid,
            distance: calculateDistance(currentLocation.latitude, currentLocation.longitude, masjid.latitude, masjid.longitude),
          };
        }
        return masjid;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance);
  }, [masjids, searchQuery, currentLocation]);

  const loadMasjids = async (queryToSearch: string = searchQuery) => {
    try {
      setError(null);
      // Fast location retrieval (<2ms)
      const location = (await getQuickLocation()) || { latitude: 17.385044, longitude: 78.486671 };
      setCurrentLocation(location);

      // Parse the query for location keywords (e.g., "in Tolichowki" or "near Charminar")
      const { searchCenter, cleanedQuery } = await resolveSearchCenterAndQuery(queryToSearch, location);

      // Optimized radius: 25km for global searches, 12km for localized keywords, 3500m (3.5km) for default nearby view
      const hasSearchCenter = searchCenter.latitude !== location.latitude || searchCenter.longitude !== location.longitude;
      const searchRadius = queryToSearch.trim().length > 0 ? (hasSearchCenter ? 12000 : 25000) : 3500;

      const nearbyMasjids = await getNearbyMasjids(location, searchRadius, cleanedQuery, searchCenter);
      setMasjids(nearbyMasjids);
      setLoading(false);

      // In the background, request fresh high-accuracy GPS coordinates and update distance
      getCurrentLocation().then(async (freshLoc) => {
        if (freshLoc) {
          setCurrentLocation(freshLoc);
          if (Math.abs(freshLoc.latitude - location.latitude) > 0.01 || Math.abs(freshLoc.longitude - location.longitude) > 0.01) {
            const freshMasjids = await getNearbyMasjids(freshLoc, searchRadius, cleanedQuery, searchCenter);
            setMasjids(freshMasjids);
          }
        }
      }).catch(() => {});
    } catch (err) {
      setError("Failed to load nearby masjids. Please try again.");
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadMasjids();
  }, []);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    loadMasjids();
  };

  const toggleMasjidTimings = (masjidId: string) => {
    LayoutAnimation.configureNext(LayoutAnimation.Presets.easeInEaseOut);
    setSelectedMasjidId(prevId => prevId === masjidId ? null : masjidId);
  };

  const handleUpdateTimings = (item: Masjid) => {
    if (MASJID_UPDATE_FORM_URL && MASJID_UPDATE_FORM_URL.trim().length > 0) {
      const sep = MASJID_UPDATE_FORM_URL.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
      const formUrl = `${MASJID_UPDATE_FORM_URL}${sep}entry.1000001=${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}&entry.1000002=${encodeURIComponent(item.name)}&entry.1000003=${encodeURIComponent(item.address)}`;
      Linking.openURL(formUrl).catch(() => {
        Alert.alert("Error", "Could not open the update form.");
      });
    } else {
      Alert.alert(
        "Update Masjid Timings",
        `To submit verified prayer times for ${item.name}, set your Google Form link in MASJID_UPDATE_FORM_URL in masjid.tsx.`
      );
    }
  };

  const openInMaps = async (masjid: Masjid) => {
    if (!currentLocation) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Current location not available");
      return;
    }

    const { latitude: originLat, longitude: originLng } = currentLocation;
    const { latitude: destLat, longitude: destLng } = masjid;

    let url = '';

    if (Platform.OS === 'ios') {
      url = `maps://app?saddr=${originLat},${originLng}&daddr=${destLat},${destLng}`;
      const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
      if (!canOpen) {
        url = `comgooglemaps://?saddr=${originLat},${originLng}&daddr=${destLat},${destLng}&directionsmode=driving`;
        const canOpenGoogle = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
        if (!canOpenGoogle) {
          url = `https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=${originLat},${originLng}&destination=${destLat},${destLng}&travelmode=driving`;
        }
      }
    } else {
      url = `google.navigation:q=${destLat},${destLng}&mode=d`;
      const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
      if (!canOpen) {
        url = `https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=${originLat},${originLng}&destination=${destLat},${destLng}&travelmode=driving`;
      }
    }

    try {
      await Linking.openURL(url);
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Unable to open maps application");
      console.error("Error opening maps:", err);
    }
  };

  const renderMasjidItem = ({ item }: { item: Masjid }) => {
    const isExpanded = selectedMasjidId === item.id;
    return (
      <View style={styles.masjidCard}>
        {/* Card Header: Name, Address, and Distance */}
        <View style={styles.masjidHeader}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 12 }}>
            <Text style={styles.masjidName}>{item.name}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.address} numberOfLines={2}>{item.address}</Text>
            {item.school && (
              <Text style={[
                styles.schoolTag,
                item.school === 'Ahle Hadees' ? styles.schoolAhleHadees : styles.schoolHanafi
              ]}>
                {item.school}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.distance}>{formatDistance(item.distance)}</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Toggle Timing Board Button */}
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.toggleButton, isExpanded && styles.toggleButtonActive]}
          onPress={() => toggleMasjidTimings(item.id)}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <View style={styles.toggleButtonContent}>
            <Text style={styles.toggleButtonText}>
              {isExpanded ? "Hide Timings" : "View Timings"}
            </Text>
            <Feather
              name={isExpanded ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"}
              size={15}
              color={isExpanded ? "#e2a23b" : "#94a4a2"}
            />
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {/* Mahmood Watch Co. Style Digital LED Board (Expands and collapses with layout animation) */}
        {isExpanded && (
          <View style={styles.boardWrapper}>
            <View style={styles.ledBoard}>
              {/* Calligraphy Header Banner */}
              <View style={styles.ledBanner}>
                <Text style={styles.ledCalligraphy}>لَا إِلٰهَ إِلَّا ٱللَّٰهُ مُحَمَّدٌ رَسُولُ ٱللَّٰهِ</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Column Header Titles */}
              <View style={styles.ledRowHeader}>
                <Text style={[styles.ledHeaderCell, { flex: 1.4, textAlign: 'left' }]}>PRAYER</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.ledHeaderCell, { color: '#00ff66', textShadowColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.5)' }]}>ATHAN</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.ledHeaderCell, { color: '#ff2a2a', textShadowColor: 'rgba(255, 42, 42, 0.5)' }]}>JAMA'AT</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Fajr */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>فجر</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>FAJR</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.athanTimes?.Fajr || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.iqamahTimes?.Fajr || "--:--"}</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Sunrise */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>طلوع</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>SUNRISE</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.sunrise || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledDisabledTime}>--:--</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Dhuhr */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>ظهر</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>DHUHR</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.athanTimes?.Dhuhr || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.iqamahTimes?.Dhuhr || "--:--"}</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Asr */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>عصر</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>ASR</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.athanTimes?.Asr || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.iqamahTimes?.Asr || "--:--"}</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Maghrib */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>مغرب</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>MAGHRIB</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.athanTimes?.Maghrib || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.iqamahTimes?.Maghrib || "--:--"}</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Isha */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>عشاء</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>ISHA</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.athanTimes?.Isha || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.iqamahTimes?.Isha || "--:--"}</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.ledBoardDivider} />

              {/* Jummah */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>جمعة</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>JUMMAH</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledDisabledTime}>--:--</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.jummah || "1:30"}</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Sahar */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>سحر</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>SAHAR</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledAthanTime}>{item.suhoor || "--:--"}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledDisabledTime}>--:--</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Iftar */}
              <View style={styles.ledRow}>
                <View style={styles.ledLabelCol}>
                  <Text style={styles.ledArabicLabel}>افطار</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.ledEnglishLabel}>IFTAR</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.ledDisabledTime}>--:--</Text>
                <Text style={styles.ledJamaatTime}>{item.iftar || "--:--"}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            {/* Sadaqah Jariyah Update Timings Prompt */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.updatePromptContainer}
              onPress={() => handleUpdateTimings(item)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <View style={styles.updatePromptHeader}>
                <View style={styles.updatePromptTitleRow}>
                  <Feather name="edit-3" size={13} color="#e2a23b" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
                  <Text style={styles.updatePromptTitle}>Update Timings</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.updatePromptBadge}>
                  <Text style={styles.updatePromptBadgeText}>Earn Sadaqah</Text>
                </View>
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.updatePromptText}>
                Help your community pray on time. Tap to submit verified prayer times.
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Google Maps Direction Button */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.googleMapsButton}
              onPress={() => openInMaps(item)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Feather name="navigation" size={16} color="#0d1a1b" style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
              <Text style={styles.googleMapsButtonText}>Get Directions</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={["top"]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Text style={styles.title}>Nearby Masjid</Text>
        <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
          Find and connect to local masajid
        </Text>

        {/* Sleek Search Bar */}
        <View style={styles.searchBarContainer}>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.searchInput}
            placeholder="Search masajids here"
            placeholderTextColor="#5c7375"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChangeText={(text) => {
              LayoutAnimation.configureNext(LayoutAnimation.Presets.easeInEaseOut);
              setSearchQuery(text);
            }}
            onSubmitEditing={handleGlobalSearch}
            returnKeyType="search"
          />
          {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={async () => {
                LayoutAnimation.configureNext(LayoutAnimation.Presets.easeInEaseOut);
                setSearchQuery("");
                setLoading(true);
                await loadMasjids("");
              }}
              style={styles.clearSearchButton}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Feather name="x" size={14} color="#94a4a2" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.searchActionButton}
            onPress={handleGlobalSearch}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <Text style={styles.searchActionButtonText}>Search</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {loading ? (
        <View style={styles.centerContent}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#e2a23b" />
          <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Finding nearby masjids...</Text>
        </View>
      ) : error ? (
        <View style={styles.centerContent}>
          <Text style={styles.errorText}>{error}</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.retryButton} onPress={() => loadMasjids()}>
            <Text style={styles.retryButtonText}>Retry</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      ) : masjids.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.centerContent}>
          <Text style={styles.emptyText}>No masjids found nearby</Text>
          <Text style={styles.emptySubtext}>Try expanding your search radius</Text>
        </View>
      ) : filteredMasjids.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.centerContent}>
          <Text style={styles.emptyText}>No matching masjids found</Text>
          <Text style={styles.emptySubtext}></Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={filteredMasjids}
          renderItem={renderMasjidItem}
          keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={refreshing}
              onRefresh={onRefresh}
              tintColor="#e2a23b"
            />
          }
        />
      )}
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: "#0f1718",
  },
  header: {
    padding: 20,
    paddingBottom: 12,

  searchBarContainer: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    backgroundColor: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)",
    borderColor: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 12,
    marginTop: 14,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    alignItems: "center",
    height: 48,

    fontSize: 14,
    height: "100%",

    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",

  },
  title: {

    fontWeight: "700",
    color: "#f3f7f6",
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  subtitle: {
    fontSize: 14,
    color: "#94a4a2",
  },
  centerContent: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    padding: 20,
  },
  loadingText: {
    marginTop: 12,
    fontSize: 14,
    color: "#94a4a2",
  },
  errorText: {
    fontSize: 16,
    color: "#ff6b6b",
    textAlign: "center",
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  retryButton: {
    backgroundColor: "#e2a23b",
    paddingHorizontal: 24,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    borderRadius: 8,
  },
  retryButtonText: {
    color: "#0d1a1b",
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: "600",
  },
  emptyText: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#94a4a2",
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  emptySubtext: {
    fontSize: 14,
    color: "#4b6465",
  },
  listContent: {
    padding: 20,
    paddingTop: 8,
  },
  masjidCard: {
    backgroundColor: "#0c3033",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
    borderRadius: 16,
    padding: 16,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  masjidHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
    alignItems: "flex-start",
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  masjidName: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#f3f7f6",
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  schoolTag: {
    fontSize: 10,
    fontWeight: "700",
    marginTop: 4,
    textTransform: "uppercase",
    letterSpacing: 0.5,
    alignSelf: "flex-start",
  },
  schoolHanafi: {
    color: "#ffdda8",
  },
  schoolAhleHadees: {
