<file path="app/(tabs)/prayer.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  TouchableOpacity,
  RefreshControl,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { getCurrentLocation, getQuickLocation } from "../../lib/location";
import { getPrayerTimes, formatTime, getNextPrayer, PrayerTimings, getCachedPrayerTimesSync } from "../../lib/prayerTimes";
import { loadSettings } from "../../lib/settings";
import { subscribeToWarmup } from "../../lib/backgroundWarmup";
import QiblaCompassModal from "../../components/QiblaCompassModal";

interface PrayerDisplayConfig {
  arabic: string;
  icon: keyof typeof Feather.glyphMap;
  subtitle: string;
  displayName?: string;
}

const PRAYER_META: Record<string, PrayerDisplayConfig> = {
  Fajr: { arabic: "الفجر", icon: "sunrise", subtitle: "Dawn prayer" },
  Sunrise: { arabic: "الشروق", icon: "sun", subtitle: "Post-fajr sunrise" },
  Dhuhr: { arabic: "الظهر", icon: "sun", subtitle: "Noon prayer" },
  Asr: { arabic: "العصر", icon: "clock", subtitle: "Afternoon prayer" },
  Maghrib: { arabic: "المغرب", icon: "sunset", subtitle: "Sunset prayer" },
  Isha: { arabic: "العشاء", icon: "moon", subtitle: "Night prayer" },
  Midnight: { arabic: "منتصف الليل", icon: "moon", subtitle: "Islamic midnight", displayName: "Midnight" },
  Firstthird: { arabic: "الثلث الأول", icon: "star", subtitle: "First third of night", displayName: "First Third" },
  Lastthird: { arabic: "الثلث الأخير", icon: "star", subtitle: "Tahajjud / Qiyam", displayName: "Last Third" },
};

function calculateCountdown(prayerTimeStr?: string): string {
  if (!prayerTimeStr) return "";
  const [hStr, mStr] = prayerTimeStr.split(":");
  const h = parseInt(hStr, 10);
  const m = parseInt(mStr, 10);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return "";

  const now = new Date();
  const target = new Date();
  target.setHours(h, m, 0, 0);

  let diff = target.getTime() - now.getTime();
  if (diff < 0) {
    diff += 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  }

  const totalSecs = Math.floor(diff / 1000);
  const hours = Math.floor(totalSecs / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((totalSecs % 3600) / 60);
  const seconds = totalSecs % 60;

  const pad = (n: number) => n.toString().padStart(2, "0");
  if (hours > 0) {
    return `${hours}h ${pad(minutes)}m ${pad(seconds)}s`;
  }
  return `${minutes}m ${pad(seconds)}s`;
}

export default function PrayerTimesScreen() {
  const cached = getCachedPrayerTimesSync();
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(new Date());
  const [prayerTimes, setPrayerTimes] = useState<PrayerTimings | null>(cached ? cached.timings : null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!cached);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [source, setSource] = useState<"api" | "masjid">(cached ? cached.source.type : "api");
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState<string>("");
  const [qiblaModalVisible, setQiblaModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  // Clock tick every minute
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => setCurrentTime(new Date()), 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  // Listen to background warm-up updates
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToWarmup(() => {
      const fresh = getCachedPrayerTimesSync();
      if (fresh) {
        setPrayerTimes(fresh.timings);
        setSource(fresh.source.type);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadPrayerTimes();
  }, []);

  const hijriEnglish = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US-u-ca-islamic-umalqura", {
        day: "numeric",
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
      }).format(currentTime);
    } catch {
      return "";
    }
  }, [currentTime]);

  const hijriArabic = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar-SA-u-ca-islamic-umalqura", {
        day: "numeric",
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
      }).format(currentTime);
    } catch {
      return "";
    }
  }, [currentTime]);

  const loadPrayerTimes = async () => {
    try {
      setError(null);
      const settings = await loadSettings();
      const location = await getQuickLocation();
      const lat = location?.latitude ?? 17.385044;
      const lng = location?.longitude ?? 78.486671;

      const { timings, source: pSource } = await getPrayerTimes(
        lat,
        lng,
        String(settings.calculationMethodValue)
      );
      setPrayerTimes(timings);
      setSource(pSource.type);
      setLoading(false);

      // Revalidate in the background with high-accuracy GPS if available
      getCurrentLocation().then(async (freshLoc) => {
        if (freshLoc && (Math.abs(freshLoc.latitude - lat) > 0.05 || Math.abs(freshLoc.longitude - lng) > 0.05)) {
          const freshData = await getPrayerTimes(
            freshLoc.latitude,
            freshLoc.longitude,
            String(settings.calculationMethodValue)
          );
          setPrayerTimes(freshData.timings);
          setSource(freshData.source.type);
        }
      }).catch(() => {});
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error loading prayer times:", err);
      setError("Failed to load prayer times. Pull down to retry.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await loadPrayerTimes();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, []);

  const nextPrayer = useMemo(() => {
    if (!prayerTimes) return null;
    return getNextPrayer(prayerTimes, currentTime);
  }, [prayerTimes, currentTime]);

  // Live countdown to next prayer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!nextPrayer?.time) return;
    setCountdown(calculateCountdown(nextPrayer.time));
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setCountdown(calculateCountdown(nextPrayer.time));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [nextPrayer?.time]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={["top"]}>
        <View style={styles.centerContent}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#e2a23b" />
          <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Calculating accurate prayer times...</Text>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  if (error && !prayerTimes) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={["top"]}>
        <View style={styles.centerContent}>
          <Text style={styles.errorText}>{error}</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.retryButton} onPress={loadPrayerTimes}>
            <Text style={styles.retryButtonText}>Retry</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  if (!prayerTimes) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={["top"]}>
      <ScrollView
        style={styles.scrollView}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={onRefresh}
            tintColor="#e2a23b"
            colors={["#e2a23b"]}
          />
        }
      >
        {/* ─── HEADER WITH GREGORIAN & HIJRI DATES ─── */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <View style={styles.headerTopRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backButton}>
              <Feather name="arrow-left" size={18} color="#e2a23b" />
              <Text style={styles.backText}>Back</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Qibla quick shortcut */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.qiblaBadge}
              onPress={() => setQiblaModalVisible(true)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Feather name="compass" size={14} color="#e2a23b" />
              <Text style={styles.qiblaBadgeText}>Find Qibla</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Prayer Times</Text>
          <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>
            {format(currentTime, "EEEE, MMMM d, yyyy")}
          </Text>

          {/* Dual Hijri Date Badge */}
          {hijriEnglish ? (
            <View style={styles.hijriContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.hijriEnglish}>{hijriEnglish}</Text>
              {hijriArabic ? (
                <Text style={styles.hijriArabic}>{hijriArabic}</Text>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          ) : null}

          {error && <Text style={styles.headerError}>{error}</Text>}
        </View>

        {/* ─── NEXT PRAYER HERO CARD ─── */}
        {nextPrayer && (
          <View style={styles.nextPrayerCard}>
            <View style={styles.nextPrayerTop}>
              <View style={styles.nextPrayerLabelBadge}>
                <Feather name="clock" size={13} color="#e2a23b" />
                <Text style={styles.nextPrayerLabel}>UPCOMING SALAH</Text>
              </View>
              {countdown ? (
                <View style={styles.countdownBadge}>
                  <Text style={styles.countdownText}>in {countdown}</Text>
                </View>
              ) : null}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.nextPrayerMain}>
              <View>
                <Text style={styles.nextPrayerName}>{nextPrayer.name}</Text>
                {PRAYER_META[nextPrayer.name]?.arabic && (
                  <Text style={styles.nextPrayerArabic}>
                    {PRAYER_META[nextPrayer.name].arabic}
                  </Text>
                )}
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.nextPrayerTime}>
                {formatTime(nextPrayer.time)}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* ─── ALL PRAYER TIMES LIST ─── */}
        <View style={styles.prayerList}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Today's Schedule</Text>

          {Object.entries(prayerTimes).map(([name, time]) => {
            const isNext = nextPrayer && name === nextPrayer.name;
            const meta = PRAYER_META[name] || {
              arabic: "",
              icon: "clock",
              subtitle: "",
            };
            const label = meta.displayName || name;

            return (
              <View
                key={name}
                style={[styles.prayerItem, isNext && styles.prayerItemActive]}
              >
                <View style={styles.prayerItemLeft}>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.prayerIconCircle,
                      isNext && styles.prayerIconCircleActive,
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Feather
                      name={meta.icon}
                      size={18}
                      color={isNext ? "#e2a23b" : "#94a4a2"}
                    />
                  </View>
                  <View>
                    <View style={styles.prayerTitleRow}>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.prayerName,
                          isNext && styles.prayerNameActive,
                        ]}
                      >
                        {label}
                      </Text>
                      {meta.arabic ? (
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.prayerArabicSmall,
                            isNext && styles.prayerArabicSmallActive,
                          ]}
                        >
                          {meta.arabic}
                        </Text>
                      ) : null}
                    </View>
                    {meta.subtitle ? (
                      <Text style={styles.prayerSubtitle}>{meta.subtitle}</Text>
                    ) : null}
                  </View>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.prayerItemRight}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.prayerTime,
                      isNext && styles.prayerTimeActive,
                    ]}
                  >
                    {formatTime(time)}
                  </Text>
                  {isNext && (
                    <View style={styles.activePill}>
                      <Text style={styles.activePillText}>Next</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {/* ─── SOURCE INFO ─── */}
        <View style={styles.sourceCard}>
          <View style={styles.sourceHeaderRow}>
            <Feather name="info" size={14} color="#e2a23b" />
            <Text style={styles.sourceLabel}>
              {source === "api" ? "Calculated Astronomical Times" : "Masjid Verified Times"}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.sourceSubtext}>
            Times are precisely calculated based on your solar coordinates with offline astronomical fallback.
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ height: 24 }} />
      </ScrollView>

      <QiblaCompassModal
        visible={qiblaModalVisible}
        onClose={() => setQiblaModalVisible(false)}
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

<file path="app/(tabs)/settings.tsx">
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, Switch, Clipboard, Platform, Alert, Linking } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { loadSettings, saveSettings, CALCULATION_METHODS, AppSettings } from "../../lib/settings";

export default function SettingsScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AppSettings>({
    notificationsEnabled: true,
    autoLocation: true,
    calculationMethod: "ISNA",
    calculationMethodValue: 2,
  });

  const [copiedDns1, setCopiedDns1] = useState(false);
  const [copiedDns2, setCopiedDns2] = useState(false);

  const handleCopy = (text: string, setCopied: (val: boolean) => void) => {
    Clipboard.setString(text);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const openDNSSettings = async () => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
      // List of settings intents to try, starting from the absolute most specific Private DNS intent
      const intentsToTry = [
        'android.settings.PRIVATE_DNS_SETTINGS',      // Direct Private DNS setting overlay!
        'android.settings.WIRELESS_SETTINGS',         // Connections/Wireless setting dashboard
        'android.settings.SETTINGS'                  // Global system settings
      ];

      for (const intent of intentsToTry) {
        try {
          console.log(`DeenAI: Attempting to launch settings intent: ${intent}`);
          await Linking.sendIntent(intent);
          return; // Success, exit early!
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn(`DeenAI: Setting intent ${intent} not supported on this OEM build:`, err);
        }
      }

      Alert.alert(
        "Open Settings Manually",
        "Your device prevented automatic settings navigation. Please go to Phone Settings  Connections  More Connection Settings  Private DNS.",
        [{ text: "OK" }]
      );
    } else {
      Linking.openURL('App-Prefs:root=WIFI').catch(() => {
        Linking.openSettings();
      });
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSettingsFromStorage();
  }, []);

  const loadSettingsFromStorage = async () => {
    const loadedSettings = await loadSettings();
    setSettings(loadedSettings);
  };

  const updateSetting = async <K extends keyof AppSettings>(
    key: K,
    value: AppSettings[K]
  ) => {
    const newSettings = { ...settings, [key]: value };
    setSettings(newSettings);
    await saveSettings(newSettings);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={["top"]}>
      <ScrollView style={styles.scrollView}>
        {/* Header */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backButton}
            onPress={() => router.back()}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={20} color="#e2a23b" />
            <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>Back</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <Text style={styles.title}>Settings</Text>
          <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
            Customize your Deen AI experience
          </Text>
        </View>

        {/* Prayer Times Section */}
        <View style={styles.section}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Prayer Times</Text>
          
          <View style={styles.settingCard}>
            <View style={styles.settingRow}>
              <View style={styles.settingInfo}>
                <Text style={styles.settingLabel}>Notifications</Text>
                <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
                  Get alerts before prayer times
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Switch
                value={settings.notificationsEnabled}
                onValueChange={(value) => updateSetting('notificationsEnabled', value)}
                trackColor={{ false: "#2a3c3e", true: "#e2a23b" }}
                thumbColor={settings.notificationsEnabled ? "#ffdda8" : "#94a4a2"}
              />
            </View>
          </View>

        </View>

        {/* Location Section */}
        <View style={styles.section}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Location</Text>
          
          <View style={styles.settingCard}>
            <View style={styles.settingRow}>
              <View style={styles.settingInfo}>
                <Text style={styles.settingLabel}>Auto-detect Location</Text>
                <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
                  Automatically use your current location
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Switch
                value={settings.autoLocation}
                onValueChange={(value) => updateSetting('autoLocation', value)}
                trackColor={{ false: "#2a3c3e", true: "#e2a23b" }}
                thumbColor={settings.autoLocation ? "#ffdda8" : "#94a4a2"}
              />
            </View>
          </View>

          {!settings.autoLocation && (
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.settingCard}>
              <Text style={styles.settingLabel}>Set Location Manually</Text>
              <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
                Tap to choose your city
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* System-Wide & Secure Folder Protection Section */}
        <View style={styles.section}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>️ System & Secure Folder Shield</Text>
          
          <View style={styles.settingCard}>
            <Text style={styles.dnsSectionHeader}>Absolute Device Protection</Text>
            <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
              Samsung Secure Folder and secondary profiles run inside completely isolated sandboxes. Android security prevents our standard App accessibility filters from reading or blocking activities inside these private spaces.
            </Text>
            
            <View style={styles.dnsAlertBox}>
              <Text style={styles.dnsAlertTitle}> The Solution:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.dnsAlertBody}>
                Configure a Secure Private DNS. Because DNS filters operate at your device's core network level, they block adult sites <Text style={{fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#ffdda8'}}>everywhere on your device</Text>—including all browsers (Chrome, Samsung Internet), Incognito tabs, and your Secure Folder!
              </Text>
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.dnsLabel}>Step 1: Copy a Trusted Filter Hostname</Text>
            
            {/* DNS Hostname 1 */}
            <View style={styles.dnsInputContainer}>
              <View style={styles.dnsTextWrapper}>
                <Text style={styles.dnsInputTitle}>AdGuard Family Shield (Highly Recommended)</Text>
                <Text style={styles.dnsInputValue}>dns.adguard-family.com</Text>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.dnsCopyButton, copiedDns1 && styles.dnsCopyButtonActive]}
                onPress={() => handleCopy("dns.adguard-family.com", setCopiedDns1)}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Text style={styles.dnsCopyButtonText}>
                  {copiedDns1 ? " Copied" : "Copy"}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {/* DNS Hostname 2 */}
            <View style={styles.dnsInputContainer}>
              <View style={styles.dnsTextWrapper}>
                <Text style={styles.dnsInputTitle}>CleanBrowsing Family (Strict Filter)</Text>
                <Text style={styles.dnsInputValue}>family-filter-dns.cleanbrowsing.org</Text>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.dnsCopyButton, copiedDns2 && styles.dnsCopyButtonActive]}
                onPress={() => handleCopy("family-filter-dns.cleanbrowsing.org", setCopiedDns2)}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Text style={styles.dnsCopyButtonText}>
                  {copiedDns2 ? " Copied" : "Copy"}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.dnsLabel}>Step 2: Configure in Settings</Text>
            <Text style={styles.dnsStepDescription}>
              Tap the button below, go to <Text style={{fontWeight: '600', color: '#d6e2e0'}}>Connections  More Connection Settings  Private DNS</Text>, select "Private DNS provider hostname", paste the copied text, and tap Save!
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.dnsShortcutButton}
              onPress={openDNSSettings}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={styles.dnsShortcutButtonText}>Open Phone DNS Settings</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Permissions & Privacy Section */}
        <View style={styles.section}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Permissions & Privacy</Text>

          <View style={styles.settingCard}>
            <Text style={styles.settingLabel}>System Permissions</Text>
            <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
              Manage device location, notifications, and other system permissions directly in Android Settings at any time without compromising your protection streak.
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.permissionButton}
              onPress={() => Linking.openSettings()}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Feather name="shield" size={15} color="#0f1718" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
              <Text style={styles.permissionButtonText}>Manage App Permissions</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* About Section */}
        <View style={styles.section}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>About</Text>
          
          <View style={styles.settingCard}>
            <Text style={styles.settingLabel}>Version</Text>
            <Text style={styles.settingValue}>2.1.5</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.settingCard}>
            <Text style={styles.settingLabel}>Deen AI</Text>
            <Text style={styles.settingDescription}>
              Your AI-powered Islamic companion for prayer times, masjid network, and mindful living.
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={{ height: 30 }} />
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

<file path="app/_layout.tsx">
// import "../global.css";
import { Slot } from "expo-router";
import { SafeAreaProvider } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Alert } from "react-native";
import * as Linking from "expo-linking";
import SplashScreen from "../components/SplashScreen";
import { initializeContentProtection } from "../lib/contentProtection";
import { warmUpAppData } from "../lib/backgroundWarmup";
import PINModal from "../components/PINModal";
import { verifyPIN } from "../lib/lock";
import { NativeModules } from "react-native";

const { ContentProtection } = NativeModules;

export default function RootLayout() {
  const [showSplash, setShowSplash] = useState(true);
  const [showUninstallPin, setShowUninstallPin] = useState(false);
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // 1. Kick off background data warm-up immediately on app launch!
    warmUpAppData();

    // 2. Initialize content protection when app starts
    initializeContentProtection().catch(error => {
      console.error('Failed to initialize content protection:', error);
    });

    // Listen for Accessibility Service blocked deep links
    const handleDeepLink = (event: Linking.EventType) => {
      const data = Linking.parse(event.url);
      if (data.hostname === 'blocked') {
        const blockedUrl = data.queryParams?.url || "Unknown Site";
        Alert.alert(
          '🛡️ Content Blocked by DEEN AI',
          `Access to ${blockedUrl} has been automatically blocked to protect your spiritual well-being.`,
          [{ text: 'I Understand', style: 'default' }],
          { cancelable: false }
        );
      } else if (data.hostname === 'unlock-uninstall') {
        setShowUninstallPin(true);
      } else if (data.hostname === 'blocked-keyword') {
        Alert.alert(
          '🛡️ Blocked Word Detected',
          `Inappropriate typing or content was detected on screen. DEEN AI has blocked it.`,
          [{ text: 'I Understand', style: 'default' }],
          { cancelable: false }
        );
      }
    };

    Linking.getInitialURL().then(url => {
      if (url) handleDeepLink({ url });
    });

    const subscription = Linking.addEventListener('url', handleDeepLink);
    return () => {
      subscription.remove();
    };
  }, []);

  if (showSplash) {
    return <SplashScreen onFinish={() => setShowSplash(false)} />;
  }

  const handlePinSubmit = async (pin: string) => {
    setPinError(null);
    const valid = await verifyPIN(pin);
    if (valid) {
      setShowUninstallPin(false);
      if (ContentProtection && ContentProtection.temporarilyUnlockUninstall) {
        // Unlock for 2 minutes (120000 ms)
        ContentProtection.temporarilyUnlockUninstall(120000);
        Alert.alert("Unlocked", "Uninstall protection paused for 2 minutes. You can now uninstall the app via Settings.");
      }
    } else {
      setPinError("Invalid PIN");
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaProvider>
      <Slot />
      <PINModal
        visible={showUninstallPin}
        mode="verify"
        title="🔒 Uninstall Blocked"
        subtitle="Enter your PIN to temporarily allow uninstallation"
        error={pinError}
        onClose={() => {
          setShowUninstallPin(false);
          setPinError(null);
        }}
        onSubmit={handlePinSubmit}
      />
    </SafeAreaProvider>
  );
}
</file>

import { Redirect } from "expo-router";

export default function Index() {
  return <Redirect href="/(tabs)" />;
}

<file path="components/PINModal.tsx">
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Animated,
  Dimensions,
  Vibration,
} from 'react-native';

const { width: SCREEN_WIDTH } = Dimensions.get('window');

interface PINModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (pin: string) => void;
  title?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  mode?: 'verify' | 'set' | 'confirm';
  error?: string | null;
}

export default function PINModal({
  visible,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
  title = 'Enter PIN',
  subtitle = 'Enter your 4-digit PIN to continue',
  mode = 'verify',
  error = null,
}: PINModalProps) {
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [confirmPin, setConfirmPin] = useState('');
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'enter' | 'confirm'>('enter');
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const shakeAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const slideAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      setPin('');
      setConfirmPin('');
      setPhase('enter');
      setLocalError(null);
      Animated.spring(slideAnim, {
        toValue: 1,
        useNativeDriver: true,
        tension: 65,
        friction: 11,
      }).start();
    } else {
      slideAnim.setValue(0);
    }
  }, [visible]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (error) {
      setLocalError(error);
      triggerShake();
    }
  }, [error]);

  const triggerShake = () => {
    Vibration.vibrate(100);
    Animated.sequence([
      Animated.timing(shakeAnim, { toValue: 10, duration: 50, useNativeDriver: true }),
      Animated.timing(shakeAnim, { toValue: -10, duration: 50, useNativeDriver: true }),
      Animated.timing(shakeAnim, { toValue: 10, duration: 50, useNativeDriver: true }),
      Animated.timing(shakeAnim, { toValue: -10, duration: 50, useNativeDriver: true }),
      Animated.timing(shakeAnim, { toValue: 0, duration: 50, useNativeDriver: true }),
    ]).start();
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (key: string) => {
    setLocalError(null);

    if (key === 'delete') {
      if (phase === 'confirm') {
        setConfirmPin(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));
      } else {
        setPin(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));
      }
      return;
    }

    if (key === 'submit') {
      if (mode === 'set') {
        if (phase === 'enter') {
          if (pin.length < 4) {
            setLocalError('PIN must be exactly 4 digits');
            triggerShake();
            return;
          }
          setPhase('confirm');
          return;
        } else {
          if (confirmPin !== pin) {
            setLocalError('PINs do not match. Try again.');
            setConfirmPin('');
            triggerShake();
            return;
          }
          onSubmit(pin);
        }
      } else {
        if (pin.length < 4) {
          setLocalError('PIN must be exactly 4 digits');
          triggerShake();
          return;
        }
        onSubmit(pin);
      }
      return;
    }

    // Digit key
    if (phase === 'confirm') {
      if (confirmPin.length < 4) {
        setConfirmPin(prev => prev + key);
      }
    } else {
      if (pin.length < 4) {
        setPin(prev => prev + key);
      }
    }
  };

  const currentPin = phase === 'confirm' ? confirmPin : pin;
  const currentTitle = mode === 'set'
    ? (phase === 'enter' ? 'Set 4-Digit Security PIN' : 'Confirm Security PIN')
    : title;
  const currentSubtitle = mode === 'set'
    ? (phase === 'enter'
      ? 'Choose a 4-digit PIN. Give this to a friend or family member.'
      : 'Re-enter your PIN to confirm')
    : subtitle;

  const displayError = localError || error;

  return (
    <Modal
      visible={visible}
      animationType="fade"
      transparent
      onRequestClose={onClose}
    >
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            styles.container,
            {
              transform: [
                { translateX: shakeAnim },
                {
                  translateY: slideAnim.interpolate({
                    inputRange: [0, 1],
                    outputRange: [300, 0],
                  }),
                },
              ],
            },
          ]}
        >
          {/* Header */}
          <View style={styles.header}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>{currentTitle}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>{currentSubtitle}</Text>
          </View>

          {/* PIN Dots */}
          <View style={styles.dotsContainer}>
            {[0, 1, 2, 3].map(i => (
              <View
                key={i}
                style={[
                  styles.dot,
                  i < currentPin.length && styles.dotFilled,
                ]}
              />
            ))}
          </View>

          {/* Error */}
          {displayError && (
            <Text style={styles.errorText}>{displayError}</Text>
          )}

          {/* Keypad */}
          <View style={styles.keypad}>
            {['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', '', '0', 'delete'].map(
              (key, index) => {
                if (key === '') {
                  return <View key={index} style={styles.keyEmpty} />;
                }
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={index}
                    style={[
                      styles.key,
                      key === 'delete' && styles.keyDelete,
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => handleKeyPress(key)}
                    activeOpacity={0.6}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.keyText,
                        key === 'delete' && styles.keyDeleteText,
                      ]}
                    >
                      {key === 'delete' ? '⌫' : key}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              }
            )}
          </View>

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <View style={styles.actions}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.submitButton}
              onPress={() => handleKeyPress('submit')}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={styles.submitButtonText}>
                {mode === 'set' && phase === 'enter' ? 'Next' : 'Confirm'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={onClose}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </Animated.View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.85)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',

<file path="components/ProtectionActivationModal.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Dimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';

interface ProtectionActivationModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  hasPin: boolean;
  onRequirePin: () => void;
}

export default function ProtectionActivationModal({
  visible,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  hasPin,
  onRequirePin,
}: ProtectionActivationModalProps) {
  const handleProceed = () => {
    if (!hasPin) {
      onClose();
      onRequirePin();
    } else {
      onConfirm();
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      visible={visible}
      animationType="fade"
      transparent
      onRequestClose={onClose}
    >
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        <View style={styles.container}>
          {/* Header Icon */}
          <View style={styles.iconContainer}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={32} color="#10b981" />
          </View>

          {/* Title & Subtitle */}
          <Text style={styles.title}>System-Wide Protection</Text>
          <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
            Filters explicit and harmful content across all browsers and applications on this device.
          </Text>

          {/* Highlights */}
          <View style={styles.featuresList}>
            <View style={styles.featureItem}>
              <View style={styles.featureIcon}>
                <Feather name="check" size={16} color="#10b981" />

              <Text style={styles.featureText}>
                Active blocking across Chrome, browsers, and social feeds

              </View>
              <Text style={styles.featureText}>
                Local on-device inspection with zero data collection or tracking
              </Text>

              </View>

              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <View style={styles.actions}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.confirmButton}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={onClose}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

<file path="components/QiblaCompassModal.tsx">
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Dimensions,
  Platform,
  Animated,
} from 'react-native';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { getCurrentLocation, Coordinates } from '../lib/location';

const { width: SCREEN_WIDTH } = Dimensions.get('window');

// Holy Kaaba Coordinates (Makkah Al-Mukarramah)
const KAABA_LAT = 21.422487;
const KAABA_LNG = 39.826206;

export interface QiblaInfo {
  bearing: number;        // Degrees 0-360 clockwise from True North
  cardinal: string;       // e.g. "WNW"
  distanceKm: number;     // Distance to Kaaba in km
  userCoords: Coordinates;
}

/**
 * Computes exact Great Circle forward azimuth (bearing) from user to the Kaaba.
 */
export function calculateQibla(userLat: number, userLng: number): QiblaInfo {
  const phi1 = (userLat * Math.PI) / 180;
  const phi2 = (KAABA_LAT * Math.PI) / 180;
  const deltaLambda = ((KAABA_LNG - userLng) * Math.PI) / 180;

  const y = Math.sin(deltaLambda) * Math.cos(phi2);
  const x = Math.cos(phi1) * Math.sin(phi2) - Math.sin(phi1) * Math.cos(phi2) * Math.cos(deltaLambda);
  let bearing = (Math.atan2(y, x) * 180) / Math.PI;
  bearing = (bearing + 360) % 360;

  // Haversine distance
  const R = 6371; // Earth radius in km
  const dPhi = ((KAABA_LAT - userLat) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a =
    Math.sin(dPhi / 2) * Math.sin(dPhi / 2) +
    Math.cos(phi1) * Math.cos(phi2) * Math.sin(deltaLambda / 2) * Math.sin(deltaLambda / 2);
  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
  const distanceKm = Math.round(R * c);

  const directions = [
    'N', 'NNE', 'NE', 'ENE', 'E', 'ESE', 'SE', 'SSE',
    'S', 'SSW', 'SW', 'WSW', 'W', 'WNW', 'NW', 'NNW',
  ];
  const index = Math.round(bearing / 22.5) % 16;
  const cardinal = directions[index];

  return {
    bearing: Math.round(bearing),
    cardinal,
    distanceKm,
    userCoords: { latitude: userLat, longitude: userLng },
  };
}

interface QiblaCompassModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export default function QiblaCompassModal({ visible, onClose }: QiblaCompassModalProps) {
  const [qibla, setQibla] = useState<QiblaInfo>({
    bearing: 292,
    cardinal: 'WNW',
    distanceKm: 4240,
    userCoords: { latitude: 17.385044, longitude: 78.486671 }, // Hyderabad default
  });
  const [compassHeading, setCompassHeading] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [sensorAvailable, setSensorAvailable] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Native animated value for silky-smooth 60fps/120fps rotation
  const animatedHeading = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const currentAngleRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const lastUiUpdateRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let headingSub: Location.LocationSubscription | null = null;
    let isMounted = true;

    if (visible) {
      loadLocationAndQibla();

      const startSensorWatch = async () => {
        try {
          const { status } = await Location.getForegroundPermissionsAsync();
          if (status === 'granted') {
            headingSub = await Location.watchHeadingAsync((headingData) => {
              if (!isMounted) return;
              const h = headingData.trueHeading >= 0 ? headingData.trueHeading : headingData.magHeading;
              if (typeof h === 'number' && !isNaN(h)) {
                setSensorAvailable(true);

                // Unwrapped continuous angle to prevent 360-degree spin flips
                const normHeading = ((h % 360) + 360) % 360;
                const current = currentAngleRef.current;
                const currentNorm = ((current % 360) + 360) % 360;

                let diff = normHeading - currentNorm;
                if (diff > 180) diff -= 360;
                if (diff < -180) diff += 360;

                const targetAngle = current + diff;
                currentAngleRef.current = targetAngle;

                // Native UI thread spring for fluid, responsive compass movement
                Animated.spring(animatedHeading, {
                  toValue: targetAngle,
                  stiffness: 140,
                  damping: 18,
                  mass: 0.6,
                  overshootClamping: false,
                  useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
                }).start();

                // Throttle React state updates to keep JS thread free and eliminate lag
                const now = Date.now();
                if (now - lastUiUpdateRef.current > 120) {
                  lastUiUpdateRef.current = now;
                  setCompassHeading(Math.round(normHeading));
                }
              }
            });
          } else {
            if (isMounted) setSensorAvailable(false);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[QiblaCompass] Sensor heading error:', err);
          if (isMounted) setSensorAvailable(false);
        }
      };

      startSensorWatch();
    } else {
      setCompassHeading(null);
    }

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      if (headingSub) {
        headingSub.remove();
      }
    };
  }, [visible]);

  const loadLocationAndQibla = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const location = await getCurrentLocation();
      const lat = location?.latitude ?? 17.385044;
      const lng = location?.longitude ?? 78.486671;
      const computed = calculateQibla(lat, lng);
      setQibla(computed);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[QiblaCompass] Location fetch failed, using fallback coordinates:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Interpolate native animated heading to needle and north rotations
  const northRotate = animatedHeading.interpolate({
    inputRange: [-3600, 3600],
    outputRange: ['3600deg', '-3600deg'],
  });

  const needleRotate = animatedHeading.interpolate({
    inputRange: [-3600, 3600],
    outputRange: [`${qibla.bearing + 3600}deg`, `${qibla.bearing - 3600}deg`],
  });

  // Check if device is aligned with Qibla (within +/- 6 degrees)
  const angleDiff =
    compassHeading !== null
      ? (qibla.bearing - compassHeading + 360) % 360
      : null;

  const isFacingQibla =
    angleDiff !== null && (angleDiff <= 6 || angleDiff >= 354);

  // Directional guidance text
  const getGuidanceText = () => {
    if (compassHeading === null) {
      return sensorAvailable
        ? "Calibrating compass... Hold phone flat"
        : "Static bearing (sensors unavailable)";
    }
    if (isFacingQibla) {
      return "Aligned with the Kaaba";
    }
    if (angleDiff !== null) {
      if (angleDiff <= 180) {
        return `Turn ${Math.round(angleDiff)}° right to face Kaaba`;
      } else {
        return `Turn ${Math.round(360 - angleDiff)}° left to face Kaaba`;
      }
    }
    return `Heading: ${compassHeading}° • Rotate toward ${qibla.bearing}°`;
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      visible={visible}
      transparent
      animationType="fade"
      onRequestClose={onClose}
    >
      <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
        <View style={styles.modalContainer}>
          {/* Header */}
          <View style={styles.header}>
            <View style={styles.titleRow}>
              <View style={[styles.iconCircle, isFacingQibla && styles.iconCircleAligned]}>
                <Feather name="compass" size={20} color={isFacingQibla ? "#00ff66" : "#e2a23b"} />
              </View>
              <View>
                <Text style={styles.title}>Qibla Direction</Text>
                <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Al-Masjid al-Haram, Makkah</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeButton}>
              <Feather name="x" size={20} color="#94a4a2" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Compass Dial Display */}
          <View style={styles.compassWrapper}>
            {/* Outer Reference Dial with Alignment Target Gate at 12 o'clock */}
            <View
              style={[
                styles.outerRing,
                isFacingQibla && styles.outerRingAligned,
              ]}
            >
              {/* Target Gate at 12 o'clock (phone's forward orientation) */}
              <View style={[styles.targetGate, isFacingQibla && styles.targetGateAligned]}>
                <View style={[styles.targetNotch, isFacingQibla && styles.targetNotchAligned]} />
                <Text style={[styles.targetGateLabel, isFacingQibla && styles.targetGateLabelAligned]}>
                  QIBLA
                </Text>
              </View>

              {/* Quadrant reference offsets */}
              <Text style={[styles.refLabel, styles.refRight]}>+90°</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.refLabel, styles.refBottom]}>180°</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.refLabel, styles.refLeft]}>-90°</Text>
            </View>

            {/* True North Indicator (orbits dial perimeter to show Earth's physical North) */}
            <Animated.View
              style={[
                styles.northPivot,
                { transform: [{ rotate: northRotate }] },
              ]}
              pointerEvents="none"
            >
              <View style={styles.northMarker}>
                <Text style={styles.northMarkerText}>N</Text>
              </View>
            </Animated.View>

            {/* Dynamic Qibla Pointer Needle (sweeps smoothly to direct user toward Kaaba) */}
            <Animated.View
              style={[
                styles.needlePivot,
                { transform: [{ rotate: needleRotate }] },
              ]}
              pointerEvents="none"
            >
              {/* Arrowhead pointing towards Kaaba */}
              <View style={[styles.arrowHeadContainer, isFacingQibla && styles.arrowHeadContainerAligned]}>
                <View style={[styles.arrowTriangle, isFacingQibla && styles.arrowTriangleAligned]} />
              </View>
              {/* Needle shaft */}
              <View style={[styles.needleShaft, isFacingQibla && styles.needleShaftAligned]} />
              {/* Counter-weight tail */}
              <View style={styles.needleTail} />
            </Animated.View>

            {/* Center Hub displaying Qibla bearing coordinates */}
            <View style={[styles.centerHub, isFacingQibla && styles.centerHubAligned]}>
              <Text style={[styles.bearingText, isFacingQibla && styles.bearingTextAligned]}>
                {qibla.bearing}°
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.cardinalText}>{qibla.cardinal}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Live Sensor Alignment Badge */}
          <View style={[styles.alignmentBadge, isFacingQibla ? styles.alignmentBadgeActive : styles.alignmentBadgeInactive]}>
            <Feather
              name={isFacingQibla ? "check-circle" : (compassHeading !== null ? "compass" : "alert-circle")}
              size={14}
              color={isFacingQibla ? "#00ff66" : "#e2a23b"}
              style={{ marginRight: 6 }}
            />
            <Text style={[styles.alignmentBadgeText, isFacingQibla && styles.alignmentBadgeTextActive]}>
              {getGuidanceText()}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {/* Details Card */}
          <View style={styles.detailsCard}>
            <View style={styles.detailRow}>
              <View style={styles.detailItem}>
                <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>QIBLA BEARING</Text>
                <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                  {qibla.bearing}° {qibla.cardinal}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.detailDivider} />
              <View style={styles.detailItem}>
                <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>DISTANCE</Text>
                <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                  {qibla.distanceKm.toLocaleString()} km
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.detailDivider} />
              <View style={styles.detailItem}>
                <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>DEVICE</Text>
                <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                  {compassHeading !== null ? `${compassHeading}°` : 'Flat'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.explanationText}>
              Turn your device until the green needle points straight up into the Qibla target. The dial will highlight emerald when facing the Kaaba.
            </Text>
          </View>

          {/* Action Button */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.actionButton}
            onPress={onClose}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Text style={styles.actionButtonText}>Done</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  modalOverlay: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.85)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 20,
  },
  modalContainer: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 380,
    backgroundColor: '#0a1d1e',
    borderRadius: 24,
    padding: 24,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.25)',

<file path="components/SplashScreen.tsx">
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Image, Platform } from "react-native";
import { useEffect } from "react";

export default function SplashScreen({ onFinish }: { onFinish: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      onFinish();
    }, 2000);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.content}>
        <View style={styles.logoContainer}>
          {Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
            <img
              src={require("../assets/splash-icon.png")}
              alt="Deen AI Logo"
              style={{ width: 240, height: 240, objectFit: 'contain' }}
            />
          ) : (
            <Image
              source={require("../assets/splash-icon.png")}
              style={styles.logo}
              resizeMode="contain"
            />
          )}
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.loadingText}>initializing Deen AI</Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    backgroundColor: "#0a0f12",
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    zIndex: 9999,
  },
  content: {
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 18,
  },
  logoContainer: {
    width: 240,

    alignItems: "center",

});
